Description: This run investigates the impact of different optimizers (SGD, 
RMSprop, Adagrad, AdamW) on the grokking phenomenon across four datasets 
(x_div_y, x_minus_y, x_plus_y, permutation). The results show that AdamW 
consistently achieves the best performance, reaching 99% validation accuracy 
significantly faster than other optimizers. In contrast, SGD, RMSprop, and 
Adagrad show slower convergence and lower final validation accuracy.

dataset across different runs. It helps to visualize how quickly each optimizer 
minimizes the training loss and how stable the training process is.
